Due to the fact that this impression has sprung up, the emotion of liking springs up, where you focus on a pleasant object and don't want to lose it. And the emotion of disliking springs up, where you focus on an unpleasant object and want to avoid it. This then forces you to collect karma. And karma forces you to spin around in the wheel of suffering life. And this is why the Buddhas have said that this suffering life is something forced on us because we have not been able to see, directly, the essence of the Ones Gone Thus [that is, emptiness]. *********"

===== root-review-targets.md =====
# Root evidence leads: C05:244–246

Withhold from independent proposal readers until both drafts freeze. These are
review targets, not accepted judgments or predetermined errata. Complete source
rows, actual prefix-query parameters/counts/raw results and full original entries
are saved beside this note. C13 is excluded from queries.

244 repeats the person, parts, no-nature and realization language with rhetorical
negative claims. Keep source owners and causal order distinct; the expanded
label of me is not all licensed by gdags gzhi. HGM8969 attests the thing which
gets the label,11734 parts to the person. zhen yul16279 has only auto-aligned
grasp, which does not mechanically license the entire apparent-object recast.
Check the full apprehension and negated inability constructions. Comprehensive
knowledge must not also claim the expanded realize occurrence.

245 gdags gzhi and btags chos occur separately with differently ordered source
phrases. HGM6486 attests thing which gets the label. Distinguish each repeated
hold, root out, inability, temporal frame, karma and power occurrence. Complete
phrases may preserve recasts, but expanded he/itself or causal content needs an
actual source owner. Exact auto-aligned sun phyung/ji srid entries are context
records, not proof that their strange string extracts are usable local units.
C16:821 shares the translation prefix but ends in a different heading; preserve
actual C05 asterisk apparatus.

246 whole root-text/commentary unit15022 and wrong-way-of-looking24849 have
binding glossary support. Pleasant/unpleasant, spring-up, karma, power and
suffering-life occurrences repeat. Keep source causes and participants intact.
HGM2953 includes bracketed Maitreya/Asanga authorship, and8522 includes bracketed
that-is-emptiness wording; presence in a glossary string alone does not license
source-absent editorial apparatus. Check full source title and essence compound,
retaining useful tighter members only where justified. Do not attach distributed
see/not-able phrasing or liking/disliking relative clauses as false atoms.
C16:822 has a different trailing heading; exact shared extent only, no inferred
independent publication/ingestion. Original self- nature typography stays exact.

## Additional targets after frozen Tibetan proposal read

The106-span Tibetan draft splits yul/object from zhen and maps245 rtog pa to
thinks. Independently assess the actual nominal/qualifier structure of
'dzin pa'i rtog pa'i zhen yul; an English thinks token alone does not prove
which source word owns it. Check second me and karma owner claims against actual
word-boundary ranges, not substring counts. The246 single final skyes is proposed
at disliking's later springs up, with liking's earlier predicate omitted as a
duplicate; evaluate the full coordination. Do not treat these provisional
choices as acceptance or as constraints on the other independent angle.

===== tibetan/244/report.md =====
C05:244 — Tibetan-first proposal by Codex; provisional machine correspondence only.
Challenge zhen/yul first: only yul→object is banked; the conceived-object qualification remains distributed. The second ma is omitted because have yet to perceive does not offer a separate negative atom.
Counts: 29 spans; 25 d5; 0 null; 0 errata. Omission reasons and every retained occurrence are in decisions.json.
Original evidence is shared at ../244/evidence/: full spine rows, full glossary/master entries, exact corpus queries/results, complete C05 ASCII source, governing originals and original-path/hash manifest.
Source-context-full-rows SHA-256: f9823a85390318c814f56a5b8271c073227ea6b51fc827800bebd3297089619b. Six selected snapshot fields independently match each reopened original spine row; neighboring C05:241–249 was read.
HGM evidence was read as provenance-bearing lexical evidence. Automatic sun phyung and zhen yul entries were not allowed to override this passage; comparative dictionary wording supplied no English.
Errata: none proposed. Full original ACIP/English, current register, and corpus parallels checked. C16:820 matches all three text fields of 244; C16:821–822 match ACIP/Wylie of 245–246, while English has different trailing apparatus. Identity proves neither independent publication nor ingestion history.
P7:12/14 provide only their preserved shared extents, with differences in wording/boundaries. Source self- nature spacing and terminal asterisks stay verbatim; no registered heading/cap class is newly filed.
Canonical final output: EXIT=0; stderr=0 bytes; body=5079 bytes. body.html is byte-identical to saved generator.stdout.html.

===== english/244/report.md =====
C05:244 — English-first, Codex, PROVISIONAL; final files frozen by freeze-manifest.json.
Challenge first: mi → never is GMR's stronger modal negation; yul → object is retained while distributed zhen/think/see is omitted.
ma in have yet to is omitted as a distributed negative. The one du null asserts adverbial fusion in directly, not absent meaning. Logical thal/true frames are omitted.
HGM master supports bdag med/no self-nature, phung po/parts to the person, and yongs su shes pa/comprehensive knowledge. zhen yul's auto-aligned grasp does not settle this local recast.
C16:820 matches all three ACIP/Wylie/English fields; id/course/seq differ. No independent-publication or ingestion-lineage inference. No cap, heading spill, or new erratum found.
Counts: 32 spans; 25 d5; 1 nulls; 0 errata. Exact per-span decisions and all omitted fragments: decisions.json / verification.json.

===== tibetan/246/report.md =====
C05:246 — Tibetan-first proposal by Codex; provisional machine correspondence only.
Challenge the coordinated skyes first: the single Tibetan verb links to disliking’s later springs up. The adjective qualifiers distribute across two English clauses and remain omitted.

Original evidence is shared at ../244/evidence/: full spine rows, full glossary/master entries, exact corpus queries/results, complete C05 ASCII source, governing originals and original-path/hash manifest.
Source-context-full-rows SHA-256: f9823a85390318c814f56a5b8271c073227ea6b51fc827800bebd3297089619b. Six selected snapshot fields independently match each reopened original spine row; neighboring C05:241–249 was read.
HGM evidence was read as provenance-bearing lexical evidence. Automatic sun phyung and zhen yul entries were not allowed to override this passage; comparative dictionary wording supplied no English.
Errata: none proposed. Full original ACIP/English, current register, and corpus parallels checked. C16:820 matches all three text fields of 244; C16:821–822 match ACIP/Wylie of 245–246, while English has different trailing apparatus. Identity proves neither independent publication nor ingestion history.
P7:12/14 provide only their preserved shared extents, with differences in wording/boundaries. Source self- nature spacing and terminal asterisks stay verbatim; no registered heading/cap class is newly filed.
Canonical final output: EXIT=0; stderr=0 bytes; body=7576 bytes. body.html is byte-identical to saved generator.stdout.html.

===== english/246/report.md =====
C05:246 — English-first, Codex, PROVISIONAL; final files frozen by freeze-manifest.json.
Challenge first: shared rang ngos nas and snang are each translated twice but banked only at the first English occurrence; shared skyes for the paired emotions is banked at disliking, leaving the liking repetition unwrapped.
HGM supports Higher Line, mental seeds, pleasant/unpleasant, wrong way of looking at things, and emotion of liking. dbang → forces is the local noun-to-causative-verb recast. Supplied authors, Buddhas and bracketed emptiness remain outside spans.
The du null marks grammatical fusion into directly. The negative n't in don't uses the established subword exception; self- nature remains byte-exact under the registered E-025/E-045 hyphen-space class.
C16:822 matches ACIP/Wylie; English ending differs (following heading versus C05 stars). P7:13 has only the opening English, P7:14 the later portion with a split Tibetan start. Neither proves an unquoted continuation or ingestion lineage. No new erratum.
Counts: 50 spans; 42 d5; 1 nulls; 0 errata. Exact per-span decisions and all omitted fragments: decisions.json / verification.json.
Complete original rows including raw, all six snapshot-field checks, master records and full corpus query results are preserved once in ../244; evidence-references.json gives exact paths and SHA-256 values.
Canonical proof: EXIT=0; body byte count=7896; stderr byte count=0; body is exact saved stdout. Invocation, unchanged generator SHA and enforced SQLite URI mode=ro launcher are in canonical-proof.json.
spec.json SHA-256: 393a8fc30127794eff026c338a5b91119c7d707008e749b7cc0ad32d9fa6353b
body.html SHA-256: 0cc8c57cd885e3c65bdbf61b20947535768807a7e95465a7385153f6b852f332
verification.json proves full Wylie/English reconstruction, exact intended occurrences, boundaries and counts. This mechanical pass does not establish semantic approval.
